# Credits and third-party assets

Every third-party asset in this repository, with its licence. Only CC0 and CC-BY assets are
allowed, and for fonts also permissive font licences such as Bitstream Vera and the SIL OFL (see
[CLAUDE.md](CLAUDE.md)). CC-BY entries and fonts must be credited on the in-game credits screen.

The game's own code, art, music and voices (everything not listed below: made by the game's own
generators and tools) are licensed under the Apache License 2.0, the repository's
[LICENSE](LICENSE) (the user's decision of 2026-10-12, concept round 35, Q13). Third-party assets
keep their own licences, listed here.

| File | Title | Author | Source | Licence | Changes |
